| lend-nas-en | en | pass | judge: The partner gave a single short English line, opened no task, wrote no terminal or install steps, asked nothing technical, and put the lend card under the message as the rubric requires. The line runs to two sentences, but that is a minor wording point. |

| todos-first-take-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent one short Chinese ack that names the action (carefully reviewing #10559 with Codex) and says it will report back, without claiming a code location, guessing a folder in its words, asking a question, or asking about taking such issues. ## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] Hi, I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else, and tell you when it's green.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어, 깨진 테스트가 있는 저장소를 맡겨 주시면 원인을 찾아 고쳐 드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 좋아요. 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたことと順番を保証し、UDPは保証せず速さを優先する**点です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama local o limpiar tus commits antes de integrarlos, siempre que nadie más trabaje sobre esa rama. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque reescribir commits que otros ya tienen da problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据完整有序地送到；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to bring it up to date and keep history linear before opening a PR. Merge when others have already pulled the branch, or when you want the record of when the two lines of work actually joined.
```

### ack-zh-estimate

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/hawdb 的 CI 又挂了，帮我查查为什么，查清楚告诉我
  [partner ack line] 好，我去查 hawdb 这次 CI 为什么挂，一般十来分钟，查清楚了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 查 hawdb CI 挂掉的原因 | place: {"kind":"repo","repoPath":"~/dev/hawdb","device":"local"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/hawdb 的 CI 又挂了，帮我查查为什么，查清楚告诉我"。 ⏎  ⏎ 任务：找出最近一次挂掉的 CI 运行，查清楚失败的原因。 ⏎ - 先看最近的 CI 运行记录（gh run list / gh run view --log-failed 等），确认哪一次、哪个 job、哪一步挂了。 ⏎ - 找出根因：是代码改动引起的测试失败、某个测试不稳定（flaky，比如依赖真实计时或并发）、环境或依赖问题，还是别的。 ⏎ - 说明这是不是"又"挂：和之前挂的情况是否同一个原因（如果能从 git log 或 CI 历史看出来）。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准：报告里给出失败的 job 和步骤名、关键的报错行（原文摘录几行即可）、导致它挂的提交或改动、根因的判断，以及你是怎么核对的（比如本地复现的命令和结果，或引用的日志行）。如果只能靠读日志推断、没有复现，要明确写出来。 ⏎  ⏎ 不在范围内：不修改代码，不推送，不开 PR，不合并。只查，不改。如果查到需要改的地方，在报告里写出建议即可，等用户确认再动手。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/hawdb 仓库。CI 完整跑一次大约 12 分钟，别在等 CI 上耗太久，已有的失败日志足够的话直接给结论。
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 在 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，也可以用关键字，Bun 会匹配文件路径：

```bash
bun test foo
```

想只跑某个用例，用 `-t` 按用例名筛选：

```bash
bun test -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the data is written down before it is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, the log lets it replay or undo those changes, so no committed data is lost and the database comes back consistent.
```

```

要做的：
- 按 PR review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 过程中的锁范围缩小（只锁真正需要互斥的部分，不要整段持锁）。
- 不改已经定下的行为：读写结果要和改之前一致。
- 跑相关测试，并把结果作为证据报告（通过/失败的数量和关键输出）。
- 改动提交在当前分支上即可。不要合并、不要推送、不要改 PR 的状态，推送和合并等用户点头。

完成标准：review 里提到的锁粒度问题都处理了；相关测试全部通过；报告里说明每处锁改了什么、为什么这样改是安全的。
范围外：不要顺手改别的模块，不要改 review 之外的功能。
```

function render(){
  var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];

    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>

      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  function load(){
    var r = ducter.get('nights');
    if (r && typeof r.then === 'function') r.then(show); else show(r);
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>

    ducter.summary('Today '+n+'/'+HABITS.length+' · best streak '+bestAll+' day'+(bestAll===1?'':'s'));
  }
  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var row=e.target.closest('[data-id]');
    if(!row)return;
    var id=row.getAttribute('data-id');
    data[id]=data[id]||{};
    if(data[id][T]) delete data[id][T]; else data[id][T]=true;
    ducter.set('ticks',data);
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function(){data=ducter.get('ticks')||{};render();});
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"habits":[{"id":"reading","name":"Read"},{"id":"run","name":"Run"},{"id":"nophone","name":"No phone after 11pm"}]}

  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-22"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>

<script>
function money(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function today(){const d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?money(total):"还没记";
  document.getElementById("total").classList.toggle("k-none",!xs.length);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔，北京出差":"记下第一笔就有合计";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    row.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent=x.what;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub").textContent=x.day;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent=money(x.yuan);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total):"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("day").value=today();
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||today();
  if(!what||!(yuan>0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = money(x.amt);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById("empty").hidden = xs.length > 0;
    ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + money(total) : "还没记");
  }

  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(function(b){
    b.onclick = function(){
      cat = b.getAttribute("data-cat");
      document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(function(o){
        o.setAttribute("aria-pressed", o === b ? "true" : "false");
      });
    };
  });

  document.getElementById("save").onclick = function(){
    var amt = Number(document.getElementById("amt").value);
    if (!amt) return;
    var xs = items();
    xs.push({
      trip: document.getElementById("trip").value.trim(),
      day: document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),
      cat: cat,
      amt: amt
    });
    ducter.set("items", xs);
    document.getElementById("amt").value = "";
    draw();
  };

  ducter.onChange(draw);
  draw();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

适合放在空状态里。它颜色柔和、造型简单，不会显得冷硬或吓人，空白屏幕也正好对应"还没有内容"的意思，比如还没有笔记、文件或记录时都能用。

有两点要注意：一是图只有 320×246，放大会模糊，最好按小尺寸使用；二是它的米色底在深色模式下可能显得太亮，要么换一套深色版本，要么在它后面垫一个浅色的底。

### call-emergency-en

```
--- step 1: user says: My neighbour collapsed, call 911 for me now!
  [partner message] I can't dial 911 for you, so please call 911 yourself right now, on your own phone.

While you wait:
- Tap their shoulder and shout to see if they respond.
- If they're not responding and not breathing normally, start CPR: push hard and fast in the center of the chest, about 100 to 120 pushes a minute. Keep going until help arrives.
- If there's an AED nearby, have someone bring it.
- Send someone to the door or street to meet the ambulance, and unlock the door if you can.

The dispatcher can talk you through CPR step by step, so stay on the line.
```

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖刚好到期那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再补一条测试：把时钟推进到正好等于过期时间的那一刻，断言此时值已经过期（或仍然有效，以现有 TTL 语义为准，并在报告里写明你采用的边界语义）；再在到期前一刻 -1ms 断言值仍有效，确认边界两侧都正确。

完成标准：新测试通过；在分支上提交；报告里附上测试名、断言的边界语义，以及跑完整 TTL 测试套件的结果。
范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR，不要改动其他不相关的测试或生产代码（除非边界测试暴露了真正的 bug，那就停下来告诉我）。
地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
```

只改 README 里的错别字（中英文拼写错误、明显的错字和误用的字词）。只改错字本身，不改写句子、不调整结构、不改格式。

在一个新分支上提交，不要动已完成的设置页那部分改动。报告里列出每一处改动（改前 → 改后），并用 git diff 核对过。

不在范围内：不合并，不推送，不开 PR；不改 README 以外的文件；不确定算不算错字的地方不要改，列在报告里让用户决定。
